" alt= " Mike Diduca is a local cannabis entrepreneur who will be applying to the province for a store operator licence on Monday . " data-portal-copyright= " Julie Oliver " **28;18603;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1533603 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ottawa entrepreneur Mike Diduca hopes to open a cannabis superstore this spring in a Montreal Road building now containing a Royal Bank branch . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On Sussex Drive at Rideau , one of the city&amp;rsquo ; s most famous intersections , plans are afoot for a cannabis boutique called mihi. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Elsewhere around town , new outlets of Hobo and Superette , two of the city&amp;rsquo ; s original cannabis stores , could pop up as well . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That&amp;rsquo ; s a hint of what to expect as the province opens up the cannabis retail trade after the initial number of shops was restricted because of a shortage of pot . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Licence applications open Jan. 6 for entrepreneurs who want to run cannabis shops . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It will be a gradual rollout , though . The Alcohol and Gaming Commission of Ontario plans to start issuing licences in April at the moderate clip of about 20 a month . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Diduca plans to call his store Budroom . He is negotiating with the landlord of the bank building in Gloucester , on Montreal Road near Shefford . At about 4,600 square feet , it could be the largest cannabis store in Canada , he says . Unless , of course , someone else opens a larger one . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Diduca says he&amp;rsquo ; s inspired by Planet 13 , a huge , glitzy cannabis store in Las Vegas that has an interactive lotus flower on the roof and LED art on the floors . He&amp;rsquo ; s planning banks of digital video walls and a conveyor belt running through the store to deliver goods from the back . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The mihi chain that hopes to open four stores in Ottawa this spring is aiming for a calm , zen design . A model mihi store built in Burlington , Ont. , features a spare design with lots of wood . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;18633;TOOLONG ... title= " A model mihi store in Burlington gives a good indication of what stores in the chain will look like . " alt= " A model mihi store in Burlington gives a good indication of what stores in the chain will look like . " data-portal-copyright= " Marcia Leeder " **28;18669;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1533667 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The corporate crew behind mihi began renting Ontario storefronts more than a year ago , when the Conservative government announced it was ditching plans for cannabis outlets run by the LCBO in favour of privately run shops . But plans by mihi and other entrepreneurs were stalled when the government announced that the rights to apply for the first handful of licences would be awarded by lottery . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The mihi chain has held on to four leases in Ottawa and plans to open stores here as soon as licences are awarded , said Steffen Schenk , a company vice-president . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Besides the storefront on the ground floor of the condo building on the west side of Sussex at Rideau , the company has rented a space at the College Square mall on Baseline Road at Woodroffe Avenue and unspecified locations in the Bells Corners and Heron Gate neighbourhoods . The company hopes to have 16 stores open across Ontario this spring , Schenk said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ottawa now has three cannabis stores : Superette , Hobo and Fire &amp;amp ; Flower . They are run by lottery winners under licensing arrangements with retail corporations that hope to expand . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hobo , a B.C.-based chain owned by the Donnelly Group , initially hopes to open 10 more stores in Ontario , vice-president Harrison Stoker said . A &amp;ldquo ; portion&amp;rdquo ; of the stores will be in Ottawa . &amp;ldquo ; I can&amp;rsquo ; t give it away , &amp;rdquo ; he says with a laugh , adding the company is negotiating leases . &amp;ldquo ; But we love Ottawa . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Superette is also scouting leases and plans to apply for licences for one or two stores in Ottawa , said Drummond Munro , company president and chief vibe officer . Its goal is 10 new stores in Ontario in 2020 , he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A spokesperson for Fire &amp;amp ; Flower wasn&amp;rsquo ; t immediately available for comment , but the retail chain with stores in Western Canada has long planned to open more stores in Ontario . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There&amp;rsquo ; s no limit to the number of people or corporations that can apply for cannabis store licences in Ontario . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; However , the initial fever pitch of excitement over cannabis retail appears to have been tempered , said Mark Asfar , a lawyer at Momentum Law in Ottawa who specializes in cannabis regulations . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The province killed some of the appetite among investors by introducing the lotteries , leaving entrepreneurs who had invested in business plans and leases hanging , Asfar said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Asfar initially had a couple of dozen clients eager to open cannabis stores in Ottawa , but only about half a dozen are still interested , he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; However , he said he was optimistic there would be several new stores in Ottawa starting this spring , and eventually maybe even some by mom-and-pop operators , since the financial requirements imposed on entrants in the last lottery are not part of the new licensing regime . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; jmiller@postmedia.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **26;18699;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;18727;TOOLONG ... drug store among first in province to offer rapid HIV test&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;18762;TOOLONG ... Transpo driver pleads guilty to molesting boys&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;18797;TOOLONG ... injured in Parkdale Avenue crash&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Jacquie MillerSat , 04 Jan 2020 00:27:10 **45;18832;TOOLONG ... BusinessLocal BusinessLocal NewsFinkelstein and Taub : A love letter to Ottawa 's futurehttp : **34;18879;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;18915;TOOLONG ... title= " Ottawa entrepreneurs Harley Finkelstein and Lindsay Taub , shown with their daughters Zoe and Bayley , are pumped about the future of this city . " alt= " Ottawa entrepreneurs Harley Finkelstein and Lindsay Taub , shown with their daughters Zoe and Bayley , are pumped about the future of this city . " data-portal-copyright= " Courtesy : Harley Finkelstein " **28;18951;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1529120 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Full disclosure : This will not be another story calling Ottawa crappy or sleepy or boring . This will be exactly the opposite : a love letter to Ottawa , containing our hope for this city over the next decade . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We&amp;rsquo ; re not from here , but we chose to live and raise our family in Ottawa . Lindsay runs an ice cream shop in our neighbourhood ; Harley just said hello to Shopify&amp;rsquo ; s 1,000th local employee . We will live here for the rest of our lives . Because of the strong roots we&amp;rsquo ; ve grown and the incredible communities we&amp;rsquo ; ve discovered , we feel a deep level of responsibility to help guide this city to achieve its full potential . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ottawa is of course working through its own issues , such as homelessness and the opioid crisis , and there&amp;rsquo ; s a lot of work to be done &amp;mdash ; but there is also so much opportunity ahead . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As we enter 2020 , what better time to set goals and ambitions for the next decade ? Here&amp;rsquo ; s one for all of us : Let&amp;rsquo ; s transform our city into the best possible version of itself within the next 10 years . There&amp;rsquo ; s a saying often heard in the Shopify hallways that goes , &amp;ldquo ; Hell is meeting yourself in the future and finding out what you ? could ? have been . &amp;rdquo ; We can&amp;rsquo ; t let that happen to our city . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There are signs that it could happen , though : Earlier this year , the NCC planned a pop-up bistro in a downtown neighbourhood . Wine and croissants in the park &amp;hellip ; what&amp;rsquo ; s not to love ? But too many people said , &amp;ldquo ; Not on my street , &amp;rdquo ; and the idea was shut down . Or perhaps you&amp;rsquo ; ve strolled by what used to be a vibrant patio along the Rideau Canal near the University of Ottawa , which has sat empty for months on end . The entrepreneurs behind these ideas , and many others , have had to endure Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s risk-averse culture every time they try to start something innovative . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The thing is , every time we say no to something new in this city , not only do we lose that opportunity , but we lose a bit of the gusto from the people who pitched it . We&amp;rsquo ; ve trained ourselves to maintain the status quo. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; What would happen if we did things differently ? What if , instead of saying no , we said ? yes ? to more pop-ups , more movies in the park , better use of our beautiful waterways , and the development of our once-iconic Sparks Street ? What if we had more street food , more outdoor hockey rinks , and more weird and wonderful experiences completely unique to our city ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;18981;TOOLONG ... title= " Let 's revitalize our once-iconic Sparks Street . " alt= " Let 's revitalize our once-iconic Sparks Street . " data-portal-copyright= " Tony Caldwell " **28;19017;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1529116 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thankfully , there are hints of this all over the city , too . Tavern on the Hill allows gorgeous views of Parliament accompanied by gourmet hot dogs . Wesley Clover Park hosts the Magic of Lights : two kilometres of pure Christmas joy that families can drive through over the holidays . During the Canada 150 celebrations , Dinner in the Sky gave us the chance to experience fine dining at a table suspended 150 feet in the air . Haunted Walks have been giving Ottawans historical tours paired with spooky stories for years . These are the kind of vibrant experiences we need more of in our city over the next decade . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We all have a role to play . Start a business , or support the people who do . You can be risk-averse , but allow others to take risks . Say yes instead of no to new things . Some experiments will fail , but each will teach us how to do it better next time , and that&amp;rsquo ; s how you build world-class cities . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The stage is already set : our local government is excited for change , our revamped National Capital Commission is committed to innovation , and Ottawa is already an incredible city to live and work in . So let&amp;rsquo ; s do this . Let&amp;rsquo ; s transform our city . Let&amp;rsquo ; s put wind in the sails of our creativity and culture . There is a heartbeat to this city ; let&amp;rsquo ; s increase the beats per minute . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This level of ambition is our wish for our city over the next decade . And if we succeed , the letter we write in 2030 will look very different from this one . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;19047;TOOLONG **26;19079;TOOLONG and &lt;strong&gt;Lindsay Taub&lt;/strong&gt; are entrepreneurs based in Ottawa , where they live with their two daughters , Bayley and Zoe . Harley is the COO of &lt;a href= " https : //www.shopify.ca/ " &gt;Shopify , &lt; ... and Lindsay is the Founder of &lt;a href= " https : //sundaeschool.ca/ " &gt;Sundae School . &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;19107;TOOLONG ... 2020 Vision -- How an Ottawa co-op aims to fight climate change&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;19142;TOOLONG ... In the new decade , Ottawa needs a new attitude toward business&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;19177;TOOLONG ... The future of city planning in Ottawa does n't look very democratic&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Harley Finkelstein , Lindsay TaubMon , 30 Dec 2019 19:22:58 **45;19212;TOOLONG ... BusinessSutcliffe : In the new decade , Ottawa needs a new attitude toward businesshttp : **32;19259;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;19293;TOOLONG ... title= " Prime Minister Justin Trudeau prepares to break ground at the Boundary Road location of the big Amazon warehouse in Orl ? ans in 2018. " alt= " Prime Minister Justin Trudeau prepares to break ground at the Boundary Road location of the big Amazon warehouse in Orl ? ans in 2018. " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;19329;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1528954 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Exactly 20 years ago , as the calendar flipped over to a milestone new year , people in Ottawa were anxious about Y2K and bullish on Nortel . So let&amp;rsquo ; s not get carried away with the idea that a year or two of business events is an easy thing to predict , let alone an entire decade . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As 2020 begins , Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s business community is perhaps stronger than it has been since the 1990s . We like to think that we earn our economic development wins through hard work and careful strategy . The arrival of a new Amazon distribution centre was trumpeted as a coup for Ottawa , but it&amp;rsquo ; s not like it would have made sense to short its shipments to Eastern Ontario out of San Jose or Boston . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The truth is that the entrepreneurial spark can ignite almost anywhere . When it does , it has as much to do with happenstance as planning . There are now no fewer than 1,000 jobs in our city , for example , that can be traced back to a German millennial meeting a woman from Ottawa on a ski trip , moving here , starting an online snowboarding company , not being pleased with the e-commerce platforms available , and launching Shopify. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That sequence of events is about as easy to replicate as dialling a random number , getting Drake&amp;rsquo ; s cellphone , and so delighting him with your unexpected call that he invites you to sit court-side with him at a Raptors game . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;19359;TOOLONG ... title= " Fiona McKean and husband Tobi L ? tke , the founder of Shopify , are shown in this file photo in front of the Opinicon Resort , whose restoration and renovation she oversaw. " alt= " Fiona McKean and husband Tobi L ? tke , the founder of Shopify , are shown in this file photo in front of the Opinicon Resort , whose restoration and renovation she oversaw. " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;19395;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1528956 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; But while we did little to deserve Shopify , that doesn&amp;rsquo ; t mean we can&amp;rsquo ; t appreciate its success . And more than sit back and enjoy the ride &amp;ndash ; crossing our fingers that it continues its explosive growth , while simultaneously hoping there are more Tobi Lutkes moving to Ottawa for love &amp;ndash ; we should ensure that the ground into which Shopify&amp;rsquo ; s seed was planted is even more fertile for future entrepreneurs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There are dozens of Shopify millionaires who will start their own companies or invest in others in the next few years . This next generation of start-ups could be crucial to the future of our local economy . Beyond that , an entire generation of digital natives is coming of age , attending Ottawa universities and colleges and getting hired for their first jobs . We must find ways to keep them in Ottawa , so they fuel our growth rather than move elsewhere for the best and most productive years of their careers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While any new employment contributes to the economy , not all jobs are created equal . City leaders have a tendency to treat growth in the service industry as though it has as much value as a new company that sells its products around the world . Our primary focus should be on industries that bring new money to Ottawa , particularly technology and tourism . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We can start by becoming a place that embraces change rather than resisting it . Unlike some jurisdictions , our city officials haven&amp;rsquo ; t banned ride-sharing services like Uber and Lyft , but they were reluctant rather than enthusiastic adopters . If Ottawa wants to attract companies developing disruptive technologies , and a generation of smart , innovative people for whom those products and services seem intuitive rather than frightening , we should be seen as a place of innovation , not bureaucracy . The local test track for driverless cars is a shining example . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Our tourism industry could also use more support . For too long , the sector has endured a pattern of two steps forward , one step back . When a new source of funding is uncovered , another program is cancelled . In addition , thanks to light rail and other infrastructure projects , the downtown core has become an unappealing construction zone . And major landmarks , including the Parliament Buildings , are less accessible because of security and restoration work . Soon we won&amp;rsquo ; t have the familiar vista of the Peace Tower to share with tourists . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As the nation&amp;rsquo ; s capital and a centre for technology , we have a unique opportunity for diverse and aggressive economic growth in the next decade . We should do everything we can to both stimulate innovation and move out of its way . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **28;19425;TOOLONG Sutcliffe&lt;/strong&gt; is an Ottawa writer , broadcaster and entrepreneur . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;19455;TOOLONG ... Making a place for all , at Ottawa 's theSpace&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;19490;TOOLONG ... newsmaker of the decade -- Jonathan Pitre : ' You could never have enough of him ' &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;19525;TOOLONG ... the News : Were the 2019 floods in Ottawa and Gatineau a taste of things to come ? &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Mark SutcliffeFri , 27 Dec 2019 11:30:15 **45;19560;TOOLONG ... BusinessNewsmaker of the decade -- Shopify : ' This is kind of blowing my mind'http : **35;19607;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;19644;TOOLONG ... title= " Tobi L ? tke , founder and CEO of Shopify. " alt= " Tobi L ? tke , founder and CEO of Shopify. " data-portal-copyright= " Julie Oliver " **28;19680;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1528118 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; **28;19710;TOOLONG : &lt;/strong&gt;Over the past 10 years we 've watched Ottawa transform , from billions of dollars invested in infrastructure to our ever-expanding suburbs . Our city 's transformation has n't just been physical . We 've been changed by tragedy , too . We 've faced floods and tornadoes , we 've faced terror and fear , and we 've witnessed nightmares on our roads . But most importantly , perhaps , this decade has also given us heroes -- people who inspired us with their brilliance and courage . These stories helped shape the Ottawa we live in today and here , in our annual newsmakers series , we return to examine the biggest stories of the decade . Today , Shopify. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;div id= " 1576082438.003200 " class= " c-virtuallistitem " role= " listitem " aria-expanded= " false " &gt; &lt;div class= " c-message c-message--light c-message--adjacent c-message--last " role= " document " &gt; &lt;div class= " c-messagecontent **36;19740;TOOLONG " &gt; &lt;div class= " c-messagemessageblocks **32;19778;TOOLONG " &gt; &lt;div class= " p-blockkitrenderer **32;19812;TOOLONG " &gt; &lt;div class= " **30;19846;TOOLONG **37;19878;TOOLONG " &g; ... &lt;div class= " p-richtextblock " dir= " auto " &gt; &lt;hr&gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt; " The spark for a high-tech recovery in Ottawa-Gatineau could well be struck by a software company you 've never heard of , in a place you would n't expect . " &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I wrote these words about Shopify and its ByWard market location in mid-November , 2011 , when the capital region&amp;rsquo ; s tech sector was still reeling from the collapse of Nortel Networks and the aftermath of the 2008-09 financial crisis . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Journalists should know better than to try predicting seminal shifts , but if you&amp;rsquo ; re ever right in this business , you should let people know . And this time we were right . At least in terms of business , this past decade was very much the story of the e-commerce technology firm that transformed from a startup into one of the country&amp;rsquo ; s most valuable firms . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Consider the size and speed of Shopify&amp;rsquo ; s advance from 2009 to December 2019 : &lt;/p&gt; **30;19917;TOOLONG surged from 15 to more than 4,000. &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Revenues rocketed from a few million dollars to an estimated US$1.6 billion . &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;The company 's market value exploded from less than US$20 million to US$45 billion . &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;The number of online storefronts built with Shopify software multiplied from fewer than 10,000 to one million . &lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; The remarkable bit : Shopify&amp;rsquo ; s co-founder and chief executive Tobi L&amp;uuml ; tke fully expected his firm would not only grow at a torrid pace , but would do so in a manner that was sustainable . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I want to build one of Canada&amp;rsquo ; s greatest success stories , &amp;rdquo ; the 31-year-old entrepreneur said in an interview with this newspaper late in 2011. &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s been my goal for a very long time . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;19949;TOOLONG ... title= " Tobi Lutke in 2011. " alt= " Tobi Lutke in 2011. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;19985;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1528112 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Most are by now familiar with the story of how L&amp;uuml ; tke and fellow co-founders Daniel Weinand and Scott Lake grew so frustrated with trying to sell snowboards online they created their own enabling software . In 2006 , they launched Shopify , which would provide all of the software tools entrepreneurs required to sell their products online . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Existing software was not designed with the new entrepreneur in mind , so we rejected the existing models and created our own , &amp;rdquo ; L&amp;uuml ; tke explains in his blog. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;20015;TOOLONG ... title= " ( L-R ) Co-founder of Shopify , Daniel Weinand and chief platform officer , Harley Finkelstein are photographed in their office in Ottawa on Monday , Dec. 16 , 2013. " alt= " ( L-R ) Co-founder of Shopify , Daniel Weinand and chief platform officer , Harley Finkelstein are photographed in their office in Ottawa on Monday , Dec. 16 , 2013. " data-portal-copyright= " James Park " **28;20051;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1528111 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; In retrospect , it all seemed so easy . In some ways , it was . The founders saw a need and moved to fill it without overthinking it . &amp;ldquo ; I never focused on the high failure rate of startups , especially ones where the founders have little to no startup experience , &amp;rdquo ; Weinand told Entrepreneur magazine in 2016. &amp;ldquo ; In a way , being oblivious to this reality allowed us to keep focused . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The founders , unusually , had the advantage of time . They had a few years to fully develop their core software platform before potential competitors could really see what they were doing . And by then , it was too late . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Shopify&amp;rsquo ; s financial performance has been remarkably steady compared with previous generations of tech stars from Ottawa . Telecom rockets such as Mitel ( 1970s ) , Newbridge ( 1980s and 1990s ) and JDSU ( 1990s ) were forever faced with shipping more hardware every quarter to keep investors happy . One late delivery by a significant customer would make a mess of revenue projections and sabotage the company&amp;rsquo ; s share price . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In sharp contrast , Shopify generates its revenues in a manner that makes unpleasant surprises less likely . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Merchants pay Shopify a monthly fee for the software platform that operates their storefront . They also shell out for add-on services ranging from shipping to processing payments . While Shopify&amp;rsquo ; s technology lacks the flexibility of custom-built online storefronts , it offers setup speed and ease of use &amp;mdash ; qualities much appreciated by entrepreneurs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;20081;TOOLONG ... title= " Shopify 's Ottawa offices on Elgin Street . The waterfall on the 13th floor . " alt= " Shopify 's Ottawa offices on Elgin Street . The waterfall on the 13th floor . " data-portal-copyright= " Ashley Fraser " **28;20117;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1528116 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The monthly subscriptions provide built-in stability for Shopify&amp;rsquo ; s revenues because merchants tend to stick with Shopify once they&amp;rsquo ; ve signed on , and the payments are automatic . Not only that , the software applications are riding a generational shift from bricks and mortar to online shopping . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Through word-of-mouth , judicious advertising and now , sheer momentum , Shopify has been adding merchants with metronome-like regularity . Once in a while , even L&amp;uuml ; tke expresses some surprise at where this has taken the firm . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; More than a million merchants are now building their businesses on Shopify &lt;strong&gt; , &lt;/strong&gt; &amp;rdquo ; he said following the publication of the firm&amp;rsquo ; s most recent quarter . &amp;ldquo ; This is kind of blowing my mind right now , &amp;rdquo ; he added . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This is not to say Shopify is immune from setbacks . More than half of its revenues depend on how well its customers are doing &amp;mdash ; so a recession would severely crimp sales . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Such a reversal would also do serious damage to Shopify&amp;rsquo ; s extravagant market value . With a share price tracking towards US$400 last week on the New York Stock Exchange and topping $500 on the TSX , Shopify is trading well in excess of where its sales and growth suggest they should be . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Yet for all of the company&amp;rsquo ; s stunning trajectory , the direct impact on the Ottawa area has been surprisingly modest . &amp;nbsp ; Consider that just one-quarter of the firm&amp;rsquo ; s more than 4,000 employees are based here at the global headquarters on Elgin Street . The company&amp;rsquo ; s Toronto-area workforce next year will likely exceed that of Ottawa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;20147;TOOLONG ... title= " Inside look at the Shopify headquarters on Elgin St in Ottawa , May 30 , 2018. " alt= " Inside look at the Shopify headquarters on Elgin St in Ottawa , May 30 , 2018. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;20183;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1528117 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; It is also an unusual facet of Shopify that 25 per cent of its workforce operates out of home offices around the globe . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With plans in place to add ever more languages to its online platform and related services , Shopify&amp;rsquo ; s spread worldwide will see its Ottawa operations continue shrinking as a percentage of its total workforce . That&amp;rsquo ; s just how it goes with successful multinationals . At the peak of the telecom boom , fewer than 20 per cent of Nortel&amp;rsquo ; s workforce was based in the Ottawa area &amp;mdash ; the home for the firm&amp;rsquo ; s global R&amp;amp ; D effort . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; What really matters for Ottawa is the example set by Shopify &amp;mdash ; not to mention the hundreds of millions of dollars its executives and employees have pulled out of the firm thanks to the stunning rise in its share price . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; L&amp;uuml ; tke and his colleagues have brought back to life the idea that you can build a global enterprise from a base in Ottawa . Just a few months before I interviewed L&amp;uuml ; tke on that day in 2011 , Nortel sold off the last of its intellectual property &amp;mdash ; the product of more than a century of astonishing corporate history . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With any luck , Shopify will carry the torch for decades more to come . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;20213;TOOLONG ... of the decade -- Phoenix : How to make sure your staff does n't get paid&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;20248;TOOLONG ... becomes terror : two crashes , nine deaths , a broken trust&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;20283;TOOLONG ... of the decade -- Confederation Line LRT : ' A new beginning for public transit&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; James BagnallTue , 24 Dec 2019 12:08:34 **45;20318;TOOLONG ... BusinessLocal BusinessNewsmaker of the decade : A decade of planning , but ' All LeBreton has ever got is words'http : **34;20365;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;20401;TOOLONG ... title= " LeBreton Flats : empty since the 1960s . Will the latest effort by the National Capital Commission finally produce a plan that will fill it ? " alt= " LeBreton Flats : empty since the 1960s . Will the latest effort by the National Capital Commission finally produce a plan that will fill it ? " data-portal-copyright= " Handout image " **28;20437;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1467829 " /&gt; **28;20467;TOOLONG the past 10 years , we 've faced floods and tornadoes , terror and fear and nightmares on our roads . Most importantly , this decade has given us heroes -- people who inspire with their brilliance and courage . These stories helped shape the Ottawa we live in today . In our annual newsmakers series , we examine the decade 's biggest stories . Today , LeBreton Flats . **26;20497;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It takes little more than 10 minutes to walk from Bayview Road to Booth St. along the southern fringe of LeBreton Flats . Turn north and it&amp;rsquo ; s just a few minutes more to the Sir John A. Macdonald Parkway . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Astonishingly this prime , 53-acre site has lain mostly fallow since the 1960s despite , or maybe because of , its proximity to the Parliament Buildings . More than half a century ago , it was cleared of gritty , industrial buildings and single-family homes to make way for a community of government office buildings . In what was to become a theme , the plan was never realized . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;20525;TOOLONG ... title= " A view of Lebreton Flats area . " alt= " A view of Lebreton Flats area . " data-portal-copyright= " Julie Oliver " **28;20561;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1459444 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Instead , LeBreton has been condemned to serve as a &lt;em&gt;tabula rasa&lt;/em&gt; for several generations of politicians and developers , a place where dreams come to die . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The National Capital Commission &amp;mdash ; the owner of most of LeBreton &amp;mdash ; has mulled multiple proposals over the years . Museums , convention centres , a world exposition , rail terminal , aquarium , housing , retail developments , with the notable exception of the Canadian War Museum , all have been promoted and discarded . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; All the ( Flats ) has ever got was words &amp;mdash ; great heaps of them , in report after report , plan after plan , each erased to make way for a new one , &amp;rdquo ; author Phil Jenkins wrote in his 2008 history of LeBreton , &lt;em&gt;An Acre of Time&lt;/em&gt; . &amp;ldquo ; More than 30 years&amp;rsquo ; worth of words , millions and millions of words , and not one serious building has been built there , &amp;rdquo ; he concluded . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Indeed , this is where things stood as the 2010s began . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The NCC&amp;rsquo ; s vision at the time was to encourage the formation of some 2,500 residential units , office buildings and a modest number of retail outlets . The ultimate goal was to create a community of 4,500 residents and 6,500 workers , all served by public transit . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But the only development of note was several hundred residential units built by Claridge on the eastern periphery . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; All that changed a few years later when three catalysts converged : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt;&lt;li&gt;In February 2013 , the city awarded the first phase of the light rail transit project to Rideau Transit Group , which would eventually lead to two new stations at either end of Lebreton. &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;On Dec. 11 , 2013 , city council revised Ottawa 's official development plan to encourage more population density along the mass transit routes . &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Mark Kristmanson was appointed NCC chief in February 2014 . Later that year , he invited the private sector to produce fresh ideas for remaking the entire LeBreton site . &lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; When the NCC whittled down five contenders to just one on April 28 , 2016 , it seemed LeBreton&amp;rsquo ; s long wait for redevelopment was finally over . RendezVous LeBreton , a joint venture between Ottawa Senators owner Eugene Melnyk and Trinity Development founder John Ruddy , would fill the 53-acre site over the next two decades with houses , apartments , a hotel , office buildings and entertainment attractions . A $600-million National Hockey League stadium would be the centrepiece . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Unfortunately , the very catalysts that pushed LeBreton redevelopment to the starting line also worked at cross-purposes as the $4-billion project entered the crucial and ultimately futile financing stage . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The LRT project , combined with the new city policy of densification , created irresistible pressure for developers to propose massive new apartment or condominium towers . Ruddy did so at 900 Albert St. , the site of LRT&amp;rsquo ; s new Bayview station , located a few steps southwest of LeBreton. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Although the city had approved zoning changes to permit towers at 900 Albert in 2012 and Trinity had submitted a more ambitious planning rationale for the site in March 2016 , Melnyk claimed in legal filings that he was not aware of the full extent of Ruddy&amp;rsquo ; s plans until late 2017 . Whatever the timing , Melnyk became convinced the marketing of apartments at 900 Albert would profoundly interfere with the economics of the LeBreton project , which depended heavily on the sale of residential and retail properties . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This commercial disagreement , among others , would erupt into $1.7-billion in claims and counterclaims late in 2018 , with the RendezVous LeBreton partnership finally dissolving earlier this year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While discordant personalities were significant influences in triggering the legal mess , the NCC appears to have significantly underplayed the commercial risk . Although remaking LeBreton would be nearly 10 times the cost and would take at least twice the time as the complicated Lansdowne redevelopment in south Ottawa , the NCC paid surprisingly little attention to the financial strength of the bidders or the stability of the winning partnership . Just 20 of a potential 140 points were awarded on the basis of ownership , management , financial capability and how the financial plan would be implemented . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The ability to work together was obviously vital . Yet it was clear from the beginning that the partnership was troubled . The day after the NCC picked RendezVous LeBreton as its preferred bidder , Melnyk threatened the team&amp;rsquo ; s project manager , Graham Bird , with legal action and termination . When trying to arrange financing , Melnyk and Ruddy wound up calling the same people , sometimes with contradictory messages . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Melnyk , as his colleagues know well , prefers to be in charge . And , because the National Hockey League stadium and associated projects were to be developed early on , he faced more immediate financial risks than Ruddy , who could afford to spread his retail and residential projects over two decades . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **33;20591;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;20626;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; The perspectives of the RendezVous LeBreton partners were also a study in contrast . Melnyk , the outsider and unpredictable pharmaceutical entrepreneur , kept his eye firmly on the commercial opportunity . Those close to him say he was also seeking recognition from a community that has not always greeted him warmly . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For Ruddy and Bird , the idea of LeBreton was epic . Hometown boys , they viewed the project as a historic opportunity to make a permanent imprint on the last large piece of open property in the nation&amp;rsquo ; s capital . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Instead , they and Melnyk will spend part of January in Toronto providing evidence to support their lawsuits against each other . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Meantime , the NCC has launched yet another effort to redevelop LeBreton . The commission &amp;ldquo ; is embarking on a process to create a renewed vision for a dynamic Capital destination and a complete community where people can live , work and play , &amp;rdquo ; the crown corporation declares without evident enthusiasm on its website. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;20662;TOOLONG ... title= " National Capital Commission 's concept map of a new plan for rebuilding LeBreton Flats . " alt= " National Capital Commission 's concept map of a new plan for rebuilding LeBreton Flats . " data-portal-copyright= " NCC " **28;20698;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1510129 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The fresh vision looks to be very much like the plan submitted originally by Melnyk and Ruddy , only the NCC intends to roll out this latest version one bit at a time . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Will it happen finally ? Will LeBreton fill up ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Maybe . But consider this line from the LeBreton Flats Plan published by the NCC 23 years ago : &amp;ldquo ; Driving the planning was an unwavering conviction shared by all that the time had come to bring life and activity back to the Flats . &amp;rdquo ; That plan , too , proposed housing , office and retail development , cultural institutions and open spaces . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sometimes , when you can do absolutely anything , it&amp;rsquo ; s difficult to do something . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The most recent decade at LeBreton remained true to the experience of the previous four . &amp;nbsp ; Nothing happened . With any luck , the 2020s will finally break this pattern . We just shouldn&amp;rsquo ; t count on it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;20728;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;20764;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Ottawa 's newsmakers of the decade were selected by Postmedia editors and by newsroom voting . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;20800;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;20842;TOOLONG ... of the decade -- Confederation Line LRT : ' A new beginning for public transit in Ottawa ' &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;20877;TOOLONG ... of the decade -- Phoenix : How to make sure your staff does n't get **25;20912;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;20939;TOOLONG ... of the decade -- Mother Nature : ' You could almost sense the climate changing ' &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; James BagnallMon , 23 Dec 2019 12:26:50 **45;20974;TOOLONG ... BusinessLocal NewsPoliticsAs Canadians await cannabis vapes , Gatineau 's Hexo is conducting more research on safetyhttp : **34;21021;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;21057;TOOLONG ... title= " Disposable cannabis vape pens sold in the U.S. are coming to Canada when regulations open up to allow new cannabis products in the country , including vapes and edibles . The dosist pens will be sold at various stores in Canada including the Fire &amp; Flower chain , which has a branch in Ottawa . " alt= " Disposable cannabis vape pens sold in the U.S. are coming to Canada when regulations open up to allow new cannabis products in the country , including vapes and edibles . The dosist pens will be sold at various stores in Canada including the Fire &amp; Flower chain , which has a branch in Ottawa . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;21093;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1525074 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Gatineau&amp;rsquo ; s Hexo Corp . says it is conducting more research on the safety of vape pens before it sells them as a wave of new cannabis products becomes legal in Canada . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Cannabis vape pens are expected to be on sale across the country in the next few weeks , along with cannabis edibles like chocolates , candy and cookies . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Cannabis producers like Hexo expected that vape pens would be as popular here as they are in the U.S.&amp;nbsp ; In some states that have legalized marijuana , vape pens have captured the largest market share after dried weed . The pens are discreet and , unlike smoking a joint , don&amp;rsquo ; t have a strong smell or release toxic chemicals associated with combustion . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; However , cannabis vape pens arrive in Canada in the midst of concern about a mysterious lung ailment linked to vaping that has put 2,400 people in hospital and killed 52 in the U.S. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In Canada , 14 &lt;a href= " https : **33;21123;TOOLONG ... of vaping-associated lung illness have been reported to the Public Health Agency of Canada as of Dec. 10 , with all but one recovering at home . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the U.S. , most of those who fell ill were vaping cannabis , especially from the black market , according to the U.S. Centers for Disease Control and Prevention . The agency says Vitamin E acetate , a thickening agent added to illegal vape cartridges , is a &amp;ldquo ; chemical of concern&amp;rdquo ; in the outbreak . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a conference call with investors on Monday , Hexo chief executive Sebastien St-Louis emphasized that his company&amp;rsquo ; s vape pens will contain no additives like Vitamin E acetate . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The liquid used in the vapes will contain only products found in cannabis , he said . &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s it . &amp;rdquo ; ( Health Canada says Vitamin E acetate is not allowed in vape pens. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hexo is conducting &amp;ldquo ; further research on safety given the reports of vape-related illnesses , &amp;rdquo ; said St-Louis. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;We already have robust product development protocols that include clinical testing , and are aligned with good production practices ( GPP ) and pharmaceutical-grade research and development . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a statement , Hexo officials said the additional testing was being done in partnership with a research organization to &amp;ldquo ; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;evaluate a number of factors : safety , tolerability , palatability and the occurrence of adverse events . Additionally , we are assessing the chemical stability of cannabinoids and terpenes ( two compounds in cannabis ) when atomized or vaporized . This will allow us to see if our compounds are degraded into potentially toxic products when atomized. " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hexo hasn&amp;rsquo ; t announced yet when its vape pens will be for sale . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A company financial report says Hexo expects to launch new products , including vapes , edibles , cosmetics and drinks made in a partnership with Molson Coors Canada , in the first half of 2020. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; However , vape pens from other producers , including major players Canopy Growth Corp . and Aurora Cannabis , will be on sale soon . &amp;nbsp ; Both of those companies say their products don&amp;rsquo ; t contain additives , either . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Canopy plans to start selling cannabis-infused chocolates and drinks first , in early January , the company has said in a release . The company&amp;rsquo ; s vapes&amp;nbsp ; are expected to be on sale in late January , starting with cartridges that come with reusable batteries under the Tweed , Twd and Van der Pop brands . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Canopy&amp;rsquo ; s disposable vape pens , including a Bluetooth-enabled recyclable version under the Juju Joints brand , will arrive later . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Both Canopy and Aurora Cannabis , another major grower , began shipping some products on Dec. 16 , the first day they were legally able to do so . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Aurora Cannabis is shipping refillable and disposable vape pens , &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;gummies , chocolates , mints and cookies . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;It will take time for all the new products to arrive online and in stores . That will vary by province , but in most cases the products are sent to a wholesaler before distribution to stores . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;21158;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;21200;TOOLONG ... study : Vaping increases the risk of lung disease by a **26;21235;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;21263;TOOLONG ... and Seidman : It 's past time to boost federal vaping **32;21298;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Ontario Cannabis Store says the newly approved products should begin to appear in stores here in early January . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Aurora&amp;rsquo ; s vape pens contain only cannabis , with no added fillers , dilutive agents or additives , said spokesperson Michelle Lefler , the company&amp;rsquo ; s vice president of communications . The vape products &amp;ldquo ; adhere to stringent quality standards , using only high-grade cannabis extracts in cartridges with food-grade internal components , &amp;rdquo ; she said in a statement . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The cannabis and hardware are third-party tested for impurities or contaminants , she said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Canopy&amp;rsquo ; s the vaping hardware is certified under a standard that evaluates the safety of the electrical system , said the company . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; " Additionally , the company is continually &lt;/span&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;reviewing and testing all inputs to ensure the highest quality and reliability of cannabinoids , terpenes , tamper-resistance , and product serialization features , " according to a statement provided by Canopy . " As an added layer of diligence , Canopy Growth leverages and stays up-to-date on safety studies s&lt;/span&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;urrounding its ingredients in order to minimize potential risk to consumers . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;We will always adapt to the most updated information&lt;/span&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; available , and we believe that we have done our due diligence required to bring the safest possible product to our consumers . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;span class= " s2 " &gt; &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Canada legalized dried weed and oils on Oct. 17 , 2018 , but the &amp;ldquo ; second wave&amp;rdquo ; of products , including concentrates , edibles and topicals , was delayed while regulations were developed for them . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the U.S , the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention has recommended that customers don&amp;rsquo ; t vape products containing THC , the component of cannabis that causes the high , especially vapes from &amp;ldquo ; informal&amp;rdquo ; sources . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Canada&amp;rsquo ; s warning is not as strong . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; While the cause of vaping-associated lung illness is still under investigation in both the United States and Canada , Canadians concerned about the health risks related to vaping should consider refraining from using vaping products , &amp;rdquo ; says Health Canada , while noting that vaping has health risks and the potential long-term effects are unknown . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; People who don&amp;rsquo ; t vape should not start , and no one should vape illegal products , says Health Canada . &lt;/p&gt;
